# Flutter parity inventory

Generated by `inventory_tool/render-inventory.ps1` from `coverage/*.jsonl`; edit the coverage files, not this table. Declarations were AST-extracted from Flutter 3.41.7 (`declarations.jsonl`); getter/setter pairs are one item. `MISSING` means no Incular counterpart was found after a search. Status meanings: `scenario` and `reading` are the methods by which the listed behavior was checked; `partial-*` means only the noted aspects were checked; `out-of-scope` items have no observable widget-layer behavior or belong to Material/Cupertino/embedder/engine concerns, with the reason in the coverage column; `unchecked` gives the reason it was not checked.
